>
  <div class="flex-auto min-width-0 js-details-container Details">
    <p class="mb-1" >
        <a class="Link--primary text-bold js-navigation-open markdown-title" href="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/commit/dbc6418357389382ee0df457a6e27ed4b8630bcf">Dev UI: always produce JsonRPCProvidersBuildItem</a>

        <span class="hidden-text-expander inline">
            <button aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Commit message body" type="button" data-view-component="true" class="ellipsis-expander js-details-target btn">    &hellip;
</button>        </span>
    </p>

      <div class="my-2 Details-content--hidden" ><pre class="text-small ws-pre-wrap">This is due to an upcoming change in Execution Model Validation [1]
where we need the `JsonRPCProvidersBuildItem` produced always, not
only in dev mode. JSON RPC providers can use execution model affecting
annotations, so we need to know about them, otherwise a non-dev build
would fail with an incorrect validation error.

[1] <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="2943296357" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/issues/46965" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/quarkusio/quarkus/pull/46965/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/pull/46965">quarkusio/quarkus#46965</a></pre></div>
